<file path="assets/js/sipn-modules.js">
/** SIPN JavaScript entry point. Semua fitur global dan halaman diinisialisasi di sini. */
import { initAppTheme } from './modules/app-theme.js';
import { initAppSidebar } from './modules/app-sidebar.js';
import { initProfileDropdown } from './modules/profile-dropdown.js';
import { initModalController } from './modules/modal-controller.js';
import { initAdminTableSearch } from './modules/admin-table-search.js';
import { initGradeTableFilter } from './modules/grade-table-filter.js';
import { initProfilePhotoPreview } from './modules/profile-photo-preview.js';
import { initProfileModalSwitch } from './modules/profile-modal-switch.js';
import { initPrintActions } from './modules/print-actions.js';
import { initPageshowAnimations } from './modules/pageshow-animations.js';

function initializeSipnScripts() {
    initAppTheme();
    initAppSidebar();
    initProfileDropdown();
    initModalController();
    initAdminTableSearch();
    initGradeTableFilter();
    initProfilePhotoPreview();
    initProfileModalSwitch();
    initPrintActions();
    initPageshowAnimations();
}

if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initializeSipnScripts, { once: true });
} else {
    initializeSipnScripts();
}

/**
 * Entry point kompatibilitas SIPN.
 * File ini mempertahankan jalur assets/js/main.js bagi template lama.
 * Implementasi aktual berada pada sipn-modules.js dan modules/.
 */
(() => {
    if (window.__sipnMainJsBridgeLoaded) return;
    window.__sipnMainJsBridgeLoaded = true;

    import('./sipn-modules.js').catch((error) => {
        console.error('SIPN: modul JavaScript gagal dimuat.', error);
    });
})();

<file path="assets/js/modules/grade-table-filter.js">
/** Filter kata kunci, status, dan mata pelajaran pada tabel nilai guru. */
export function initGradeTableFilter() {
    const table = document.getElementById('gradeTable');
    if (!table) return;
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('gradeSearch');
    const statusFilter = document.getElementById('gradeStatusFilter');
    const subjectFilter = document.getElementById('gradeSubjectFilter');
    const resetButton = document.getElementById('gradeFilterReset');
    const resultCount = document.getElementById('gradeResultCount');
    const noResults = document.getElementById('gradeNoResults');
    const rows = Array.from(table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr[data-grade-row]'));
    const totalRows = rows.length;

    const applyFilters = () => {
        const keyword = (searchInput?.value || '').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('id');
        const status = (statusFilter?.value || '').toLocaleLowerCase('id');
        const subject = subjectFilter?.value || '';
        let visibleRows = 0;
        rows.forEach((row) => {
            const searchText = (row.dataset.search || '').toLocaleLowerCase('id');
            const rowStatus = (row.dataset.status || '').toLocaleLowerCase('id');
            const rowSubject = row.dataset.mapel || '';
            const matches = (!keyword || searchText.includes(keyword))
                && (!status || rowStatus === status)
                && (!subject || rowSubject === subject);
            row.hidden = !matches;
            if (matches) visibleRows += 1;
        });
        if (noResults) noResults.hidden = totalRows === 0 || visibleRows > 0;
        if (resultCount) resultCount.textContent = `Menampilkan ${visibleRows.toLocaleString('id-ID')} dari ${totalRows.toLocaleString('id-ID')} data`;
    };

    searchInput?.addEventListener('input', applyFilters);
    statusFilter?.addEventListener('change', applyFilters);
    subjectFilter?.addEventListener('change', applyFilters);
    resetButton?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';
        if (statusFilter) statusFilter.value = '';
        if (subjectFilter) subjectFilter.value = '';
        applyFilters();
        searchInput?.focus();
    });
    applyFilters();
}

<file path="assets/js/modules/admin-table-search.js">
/**
 * Pencarian tabel admin.
 * Kontrak HTML: [data-admin-search-table], [data-admin-table-search],
 * [data-admin-search-row], [data-admin-search-clear], [data-admin-results-count],
 * [data-admin-search-empty], dan opsional [data-search-ignore].
 */
export function initAdminTableSearch() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-admin-search-table]').forEach((table) => {
        const page = table.closest('.sipn-admin-data-page') || document;
        const searchInput = page.querySelector('[data-admin-table-search]');
        const clearButton = page.querySelector('[data-admin-search-clear]');
        const resultCount = page.querySelector('[data-admin-results-count]');
        const emptyState = page.querySelector('[data-admin-search-empty]');
        const rows = Array.from(table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr[data-admin-search-row]'));
        const totalRows = rows.length;
        if (!searchInput) return;

        const normalize = (value) => String(value ?? '')
            .toLocaleLowerCase('id')
            .normalize('NFD')
            .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
            .trim();
        const rowText = (row) => row.dataset.search || Array.from(row.cells)
            .filter((cell) => !cell.hasAttribute('data-search-ignore'))
            .map((cell) => cell.textContent || '')
            .join(' ');
        const indexedRows = rows.map((row) => ({ row, text: normalize(rowText(row)) }));

        const applyFilter = () => {
            const keyword = normalize(searchInput.value);
            let visibleRows = 0;
            indexedRows.forEach(({ row, text }) => {
                const matches = keyword === '' || text.includes(keyword);
                row.hidden = !matches;
                if (matches) visibleRows += 1;
            });
            if (resultCount) resultCount.textContent = visibleRows.toLocaleString('id-ID');
            if (emptyState) emptyState.hidden = totalRows === 0 || visibleRows > 0;
        };

        searchInput.addEventListener('input', applyFilter);
        clearButton?.addEventListener('click', () => {
            searchInput.value = '';
            applyFilter();
            searchInput.focus();
        });
        applyFilter();
    });
}

<file path="assets/js/modules/modal-controller.js">
/** Controller modal global SIPN: buka, tutup, Escape, backdrop, dan focus trap. */
const openerByModal = new WeakMap();
let previousBodyOverflow = '';
let bodyLockActive = false;

const FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR = [
    'a[href]',
    'area[href]',
    'button:not([disabled])',
    'input:not([disabled]):not([type="hidden"])',
    'select:not([disabled])',
    'textarea:not([disabled])',
    '[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
].join(',');

function getFocusableElements(modal) {
    return Array.from(modal.querySelectorAll(FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR)).filter((element) => {
        return !element.hidden && element.getAttribute('aria-hidden') !== 'true'
            && element.getClientRects().length > 0;
    });
}

function syncBodyLock() {
    const hasOpenModal = Boolean(document.querySelector('.modal-overlay.is-active'));
    if (hasOpenModal && !bodyLockActive) {
        previousBodyOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        bodyLockActive = true;
    } else if (!hasOpenModal && bodyLockActive) {
        document.body.style.overflow = previousBodyOverflow;
        bodyLockActive = false;
    }
}

export function openSipnModal(modal, opener = document.activeElement) {
    if (!(modal instanceof HTMLElement) || !modal.classList.contains('modal-overlay')) return false;

    document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay.is-active').forEach((activeModal) => {
        if (activeModal === modal) return;
        activeModal.classList.remove('is-active');
        activeModal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    });

    if (opener instanceof HTMLElement) openerByModal.set(modal, opener);
    modal.classList.add('is-active');
    modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');

    const dialog = modal.querySelector('.modal-box');
    if (dialog) {
        if (!dialog.hasAttribute('role')) dialog.setAttribute('role', 'dialog');
        if (!dialog.hasAttribute('tabindex')) dialog.setAttribute('tabindex', '-1');
        dialog.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
    }

    syncBodyLock();
    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (!modal.classList.contains('is-active')) return;
        const focusable = getFocusableElements(modal);
        if (focusable.length) focusable[0].focus({ preventScroll: true });
        else dialog?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    });
    return true;
}

export function closeSipnModal(modal, { restoreFocus = true } = {}) {
    if (!(modal instanceof HTMLElement) || !modal.classList.contains('modal-overlay')) return false;

    modal.classList.remove('is-active');
    modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    const opener = openerByModal.get(modal);
    openerByModal.delete(modal);
    syncBodyLock();

    if (restoreFocus && opener?.isConnected) {
        window.requestAnimationFrame(() => opener.focus({ preventScroll: true }));
    }
    return true;
}

export function switchSipnModal(sourceModal, targetModal, trigger) {
    if (!sourceModal || !targetModal) return false;
    const originalOpener = openerByModal.get(sourceModal) || trigger || document.activeElement;
    closeSipnModal(sourceModal, { restoreFocus: false });
    return openSipnModal(targetModal, originalOpener);
}

export function initModalController() {
    // Tetapkan keadaan aksesibilitas awal bagi semua modal pada halaman.
    document.querySelectorAll('.modal-overlay').forEach((modal) => {
        if (!modal.classList.contains('is-active')) modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    });

    document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
        const clickTarget = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target : null;
        if (!clickTarget) return;

        const openTrigger = clickTarget.closest('[data-modal-target]');
        if (openTrigger) {
            const selector = openTrigger.getAttribute('data-modal-target');
            let modal = null;
            try {
                modal = selector ? document.querySelector(selector) : null;
            } catch (error) {
                modal = null;
            }
            if (modal?.classList.contains('modal-overlay')) {
                event.preventDefault();
                openSipnModal(modal, openTrigger);
                return;
            }
        }

        const closeTrigger = clickTarget.closest('[data-modal-close]');
        if (closeTrigger) {
            const modal = closeTrigger.closest('.modal-overlay');
            if (modal) {
                event.preventDefault();
                closeSipnModal(modal);
            }
            return;
        }

        if (clickTarget.classList.contains('modal-overlay')) {
            closeSipnModal(clickTarget);
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        const activeModal = document.querySelector('.modal-overlay.is-active');
        if (!activeModal) return;

        if (event.key === 'Escape') {
            event.preventDefault();
            closeSipnModal(activeModal);
            return;
        }

        if (event.key !== 'Tab') return;
        const focusable = getFocusableElements(activeModal);
        if (!focusable.length) {
            event.preventDefault();
            activeModal.querySelector('.modal-box')?.focus();
            return;
        }

        const first = focusable[0];
        const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
        if (!activeModal.contains(document.activeElement)) {
            event.preventDefault();
            first.focus();
        } else if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
            event.preventDefault();
            last.focus();
        } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
            event.preventDefault();
            first.focus();
        }
    });
}

<file path="assets/js/modules/profile-modal-switch.js">
/** Peralihan dari modal pilihan profil ke modal edit data/foto. */
import { switchSipnModal } from './modal-controller.js';

export function initProfileModalSwitch() {
    const activate = (trigger) => {
        const selector = trigger.dataset.openModal;
        const sourceModal = document.getElementById('modalOpsiEdit');
        let targetModal = null;
        try {
            targetModal = selector ? document.querySelector(selector) : null;
        } catch (error) {
            targetModal = null;
        }
        if (!sourceModal || !targetModal?.classList.contains('modal-overlay')) return;
        switchSipnModal(sourceModal, targetModal, trigger);
    };

    document.querySelectorAll('[data-profile-modal-switch]').forEach((trigger) => {
        trigger.addEventListener('click', () => activate(trigger));
        trigger.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
            if (event.key !== 'Enter' && event.key !== ' ') return;
            event.preventDefault();
            activate(trigger);
        });
    });
}

</file>

<file path="assets/js/modules/profile-photo-preview.js">
/** Pratinjau foto profil sebelum formulir diunggah. */
export function initProfilePhotoPreview() {
    const input = document.querySelector('[data-profile-photo-input]');
    if (!input) return;
    const initialContent = document.getElementById('dropzoneContentAwal');
    const previewWrapper = document.getElementById('wadahPreview');
    const previewImage = document.getElementById('gambarPreview');
    const fileBadge = document.getElementById('badgeNamaFile');
    const fileName = document.getElementById('teksNamaFile');
    if (!initialContent || !previewWrapper || !previewImage || !fileBadge || !fileName) return;

    const resetPreview = () => {
        initialContent.style.display = 'block';
        previewWrapper.style.display = 'none';
        fileBadge.style.display = 'none';
        previewImage.removeAttribute('src');
        fileName.textContent = '-';
    };

    input.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const file = input.files?.[0];
        if (!file) { resetPreview(); return; }
        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
            input.value = '';
            resetPreview();
            return;
        }
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.addEventListener('load', () => {
            if (typeof reader.result !== 'string') return;
            initialContent.style.display = 'none';
            previewWrapper.style.display = 'block';
            previewImage.src = reader.result;
            fileBadge.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            fileName.textContent = file.name;
        });
        reader.addEventListener('error', () => { input.value = ''; resetPreview(); });
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
}

</file>

<file path="assets/js/modules/app-theme.js">
/** Mengelola tema terang/gelap SIPN dan menyimpan preferensi pengguna. */
const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'sipn-theme';
const VALID_THEMES = new Set(['light', 'dark']);

function readSavedTheme() {
    try {
        const savedTheme = window.localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY);
        return VALID_THEMES.has(savedTheme) ? savedTheme : 'light';
    } catch (error) {
        // Aplikasi tetap berjalan bila penyimpanan browser dinonaktifkan.
        return 'light';
    }
}

function applyTheme(theme) {
    const nextTheme = VALID_THEMES.has(theme) ? theme : 'light';
    const isDark = nextTheme === 'dark';
    const root = document.documentElement;
    const body = document.body;

    root.dataset.theme = nextTheme;
    if (body) body.dataset.theme = nextTheme;

    const toggle = document.getElementById('themeToggleSidebar');
    if (toggle) {
        const icon = toggle.querySelector('i');
        const label = document.getElementById('themeText');

        toggle.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(isDark));
        toggle.setAttribute('aria-label', isDark ? 'Aktifkan tema terang' : 'Aktifkan tema gelap');
        if (label) label.textContent = isDark ? 'Light Mode' : 'Dark Mode';
        if (icon) {
            icon.classList.toggle('bi-moon-stars-fill', !isDark);
            icon.classList.toggle('bi-sun-fill', isDark);
        }
    }

    const themeColor = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    if (themeColor) themeColor.setAttribute('content', isDark ? '#0f172a' : '#1e3a8a');
}

export function initAppTheme() {
    if (!document.body) return;

    applyTheme(readSavedTheme());

    const toggle = document.getElementById('themeToggleSidebar');
    toggle?.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const nextTheme = document.body.dataset.theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
        applyTheme(nextTheme);
        try {
            window.localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, nextTheme);
        } catch (error) {
            // Tema tetap berubah untuk halaman aktif walau preferensi tidak dapat disimpan.
        }
    });

    // Sinkronkan tema bila preferensi berubah dari tab SIPN lain.
    window.addEventListener('storage', (event) => {
        if (event.key === THEME_STORAGE_KEY && VALID_THEMES.has(event.newValue)) {
            applyTheme(event.newValue);
        }
    });
}

</file>

<file path="assets/js/modules/app-sidebar.js">
/** Interaksi sidebar: rail tablet, drawer mobile, backdrop, dan tombol Escape. */
const MOBILE_QUERY = '(max-width: 768px)';
const TABLET_QUERY = '(min-width: 769px) and (max-width: 1199px)';

export function initAppSidebar() {
    const body = document.body;
    const sidebar = document.getElementById('appSidebar');
    const toggle = document.getElementById('sidebarToggle');
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('sidebarBackdrop');
    if (!body || !sidebar || !toggle) return;

    const mobileQuery = window.matchMedia(MOBILE_QUERY);
    const tabletQuery = window.matchMedia(TABLET_QUERY);

    const syncAria = () => {
        const isMobile = mobileQuery.matches;
        const isTablet = tabletQuery.matches;
        const isOpen = isMobile
            ? body.classList.contains('sidebar-open')
            : isTablet && body.classList.contains('sidebar-expanded');

        toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(isOpen));
        toggle.setAttribute('aria-label', isOpen ? 'Tutup menu navigasi' : 'Buka menu navigasi');
        sidebar.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(isMobile && !isOpen));

        if (backdrop) {
            const visible = isMobile && isOpen;
            backdrop.classList.toggle('is-visible', visible);
            backdrop.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(!visible));
            backdrop.tabIndex = visible ? 0 : -1;
        }
    };

    const closeSidebar = ({ restoreFocus = false } = {}) => {
        body.classList.remove('sidebar-open', 'sidebar-expanded');
        syncAria();
        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('sipn:sidebar-close'));
        if (restoreFocus) toggle.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    };

    const openSidebar = () => {
        if (mobileQuery.matches) {
            body.classList.add('sidebar-open');
            syncAria();
            const firstLink = sidebar.querySelector('.sidebar-menu-container a.nav-link, .sidebar-menu-container button.nav-link');
            firstLink?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
        } else if (tabletQuery.matches) {
            body.classList.add('sidebar-expanded');
            syncAria();
        }
    };

    toggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isOpen = mobileQuery.matches
            ? body.classList.contains('sidebar-open')
            : tabletQuery.matches && body.classList.contains('sidebar-expanded');
        if (isOpen) closeSidebar({ restoreFocus: true });
        else openSidebar();
    });

    backdrop?.addEventListener('click', () => closeSidebar({ restoreFocus: true }));

    sidebar.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
        const link = event.target.closest('a');
        if (link && mobileQuery.matches) closeSidebar();
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.key !== 'Escape') return;
        if (body.classList.contains('sidebar-open') || body.classList.contains('sidebar-expanded')) {
            closeSidebar({ restoreFocus: true });
        }
    });

    const handleViewportChange = () => {
        // State mobile dan tablet tidak dibawa ke breakpoint lain.
        body.classList.remove('sidebar-open', 'sidebar-expanded');
        syncAria();
    };

    if (typeof mobileQuery.addEventListener === 'function') {
        mobileQuery.addEventListener('change', handleViewportChange);
        tabletQuery.addEventListener('change', handleViewportChange);
    } else {
        // Fallback untuk browser lama yang masih memakai addListener.
        mobileQuery.addListener(handleViewportChange);
        tabletQuery.addListener(handleViewportChange);
    }

    syncAria();
}

</file>

<file path="assets/js/modules/profile-dropdown.js">
/** Dropdown menu profil pada bagian bawah sidebar. */
export function initProfileDropdown() {
    const trigger = document.getElementById('profileTriggerBtn');
    const dropdown = document.getElementById('profileDropdown');
    if (!trigger || !dropdown) return;

    const setOpen = (open, { restoreFocus = false } = {}) => {
        dropdown.classList.toggle('is-open', open);
        dropdown.setAttribute('aria-hidden', String(!open));
        trigger.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
        if (restoreFocus) trigger.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    };

    // Pastikan status awal sesuai markup/CSS.
    setOpen(false);

    trigger.addEventListener('click', () => {
        setOpen(!dropdown.classList.contains('is-open'));
    });

    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => {
        if (!dropdown.classList.contains('is-open')) return;
        if (trigger.contains(event.target) || dropdown.contains(event.target)) return;
        setOpen(false);
    });

    dropdown.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
        if (event.target.closest('a')) setOpen(false);
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Escape' && dropdown.classList.contains('is-open')) {
            setOpen(false, { restoreFocus: true });
        }
    });

    document.addEventListener('sipn:sidebar-close', () => setOpen(false));
}

</file>

<file path="assets/js/modules/pageshow-animations.js">
/** Pemulihan animasi saat halaman dikembalikan dari back-forward cache. */
export function initPageshowAnimations() {
    window.addEventListener('pageshow', (event) => {
        if (!event.persisted) return;
        const body = document.body;
        if (body?.classList.contains('sipn-dashboard-stagger')) {
            body.classList.remove('sipn-dashboard-stagger');
            void body.offsetWidth;
            body.classList.add('sipn-dashboard-stagger');
        }
        const historyPage = document.querySelector('.sipn-admin-data-page--history');
        if (!historyPage || window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return;
        const animated = historyPage.querySelectorAll('.sipn-admin-data-enter, .sipn-admin-history-table tbody tr[data-admin-search-row]');
        animated.forEach((element) => { element.style.animation = 'none'; });
        void historyPage.offsetHeight;
        animated.forEach((element) => { element.style.removeProperty('animation'); });
    });
}

</file>

    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto minmax(0, 1fr);
    align-items: center;
    gap: clamp(0.75rem, 1.5vw, 1.5rem);

/* SIPN CSS MODULE: Interaksi, aksesibilitas, dan animasi antarmuka. Keep import order in style.css. */

/* =========================================================
   AUTHENTICATION PAGES — SUBTLE HOVER INTERACTIONS
   Tidak ada animasi otomatis saat halaman dibuka.
   Efek hanya berjalan ketika elemen di-hover atau difokuskan.
   ========================================================= */

/* Tombol utama: sedikit terangkat dengan transisi halus */
.auth-page .auth-btn-submit {
  transition:
    background-color 180ms ease,
    box-shadow 180ms ease,
    transform 180ms ease;
  will-change: transform;
}

.auth-page .auth-btn-submit:hover {
  background: var(--primary-hover);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 7px 16px rgba(30, 58, 138, 0.18);
}

.auth-page .auth-btn-submit:hover i {
  transform: translateX(3px);
}

.auth-page .auth-btn-submit i {
  transition: transform 180ms ease;
}

.auth-page .auth-btn-submit:active {
  background: var(--primary-dark);
  transform: translateY(0);
  box-shadow: none;
}

/* Link: perubahan warna dan garis bawah tidak mendadak */
.auth-page a {
  transition: color 160ms ease, text-decoration-color 160ms ease;
}

.auth-page a:hover {
  color: var(--primary-hover) !important;
  text-decoration: underline !important;
}

/* Input: fokus lebih jelas, tanpa gerakan berlebihan */
.auth-page .auth-input-wrapper input,
.auth-page .auth-input-wrapper select {
  transition: border-color 160ms ease, box-shadow 160ms ease;
}

.auth-page .auth-input-wrapper input:focus,
.auth-page .auth-input-wrapper select:focus {
  border-color: var(--primary-dark);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(30, 58, 138, 0.10);
}

/* Fokus keyboard tetap terlihat */
.auth-page button:focus-visible,
.auth-page a:focus-visible,
.auth-page input:focus-visible,
.auth-page select:focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--primary-hover);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

/* Hormati pengaturan pengurangan gerakan perangkat */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .auth-page .auth-btn-submit,
  .auth-page .auth-btn-submit i,
  .auth-page a,
  .auth-page .auth-input-wrapper input,
  .auth-page .auth-input-wrapper select {
    transition: none !important;
  }

  .auth-page .auth-btn-submit:hover,
  .auth-page .auth-btn-submit:active,
  .auth-page .auth-btn-submit:hover i {
    transform: none !important;
  }
}

/*
 * Staggered entrance: elemen dashboard tampil berurutan.
 * Class dipasang oleh template_header.php pada semua halaman dashboard.
 */
@keyframes sipnStaggerEntrance {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(13px) scale(0.99);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0) scale(1);
  }
}

<file path="index.php">
<?php
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}
require_once __DIR__ . '/config/koneksi_database.php';

// Jika pengguna SUDAH login, langsung lempar ke dashboard masing-masing
if (isset($_SESSION['role'])) {
    switch ($_SESSION['role']) {
        case 'admin':
            header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "admin/dashboard_admin.php");
            exit();
        case 'guru':
            header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "guru/dashboard_guru.php");
            exit();
        case 'siswa':
            header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "siswa/dashboard_siswa.php");
            exit();
    }
}

// Jika BELUM login, alihkan ke form login
header("Location: " . BASE_URL . "auth/form_login.php");
exit();
?>
</file>

<?php
session_start();
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';

if (isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    $role = $_SESSION['role'];
    header("Location: " . BASE_URL . $role . "/dashboard_" . $role . ".php");
    exit();
}

$error = '';

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $username = trim($_POST['username'] ?? '');
    $password = trim($_POST['password'] ?? '');

    if (empty($username) || empty($password)) {
        $error = 'Username dan Password wajib diisi!';
    } else {
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "SELECT id, username, password, role FROM users WHERE username = ?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "s", $username);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt);
        $result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt);

        if ($user = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
            if (password_verify($password, $user['password'])) {
                // Regenerasi ID session setelah autentikasi berhasil.
                session_regenerate_id(true);

                $_SESSION['user_id']  = $user['id'];
                $_SESSION['username'] = $user['username'];
                $_SESSION['role']     = $user['role'];
                $_SESSION['sipn_dashboard_entry'] = true;

                set_flash_message('success', 'Selamat datang kembali, ' . $user['username'] . '!');

                header("Location: " . BASE_URL . $user['role'] . "/dashboard_" . $user['role'] . ".php");
                exit();
            } else {
                $error = 'Password yang Anda masukkan salah!';
            }
        } else {
            $error = 'Username tidak ditemukan!';
        }
        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
    }
}

require __DIR__ . '/views/form_login_view.php';

</file>

<file path="auth/proses_logout.php">
<?php
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';

$session_name = session_name();

// Hapus seluruh data autentikasi dari session yang sedang aktif.
$_SESSION = [];

// Hapus cookie session lama dari browser.
if (ini_get('session.use_cookies')) {
    $params = session_get_cookie_params();
    setcookie(
        $session_name,
        '',
        time() - 42000,
        $params['path'],
        $params['domain'],
        $params['secure'],
        $params['httponly']
    );
}

// Akhiri session autentikasi lama.
session_destroy();

// Buat session anonim baru agar flash message tidak memakai ID session lama.
if (isset($_COOKIE[$session_name])) {
    unset($_COOKIE[$session_name]);
}
session_start();
session_regenerate_id(true);
set_flash_message('success', 'Anda berhasil logout dari SIPN. Sampai jumpa!');

header('Location: ' . BASE_URL . 'auth/form_login.php');
exit();
</file>

<file path="auth/form_register_siswa.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $username = sanitize($_POST['username']);
    $password = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
    $nis      = sanitize($_POST['nis']);
    $nama     = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
    $kelas    = sanitize($_POST['kelas']);
    $jk       = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);

    mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
    try {
        $stmt1 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, role) VALUES (?, ?, 'siswa')");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt1, "ss", $username, $password);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt1);
        $user_id = mysqli_insert_id($conn);

        $stmt2 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO siswa (nis, nama, kelas, jenis_kelamin, user_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt2, "ssssi", $nis, $nama, $kelas, $jk, $user_id);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt2);

        mysqli_commit($conn);
        set_flash_message('success', 'Registrasi berhasil! Silakan login.');
        header('Location: ' . BASE_URL . 'auth/form_login.php');
        exit();
    } catch (Throwable $e) {
        mysqli_rollback($conn);
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal registrasi! NIS atau Username mungkin sudah terdaftar.');
    }
}

require __DIR__ . '/views/form_register_siswa_view.php';

</file>

<file path="guru/data_nilai_siswa.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';

check_role(['guru']);

$user_id = (int) ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
$id_guru = 0;
$nama_guru = 'Guru';
$guru_error = false;

$escape = static function ($value) {
    return htmlspecialchars((string) ($value ?? ''), ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
};

// CSRF token khusus formulir pengelolaan nilai pada halaman ini.
if (empty($_SESSION['sipn_grade_csrf'])) {
    $_SESSION['sipn_grade_csrf'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
}
$csrf_token = $_SESSION['sipn_grade_csrf'];

// Ambil identitas guru berdasarkan akun yang sedang aktif.
$stmt_guru = $conn->prepare('SELECT id, nama FROM guru WHERE user_id = ? LIMIT 1');
if ($stmt_guru) {
    $stmt_guru->bind_param('i', $user_id);
    $stmt_guru->execute();
    $result_guru = $stmt_guru->get_result();
    $data_guru = $result_guru ? $result_guru->fetch_assoc() : null;
    $stmt_guru->close();

    if ($data_guru) {
        $id_guru = (int) $data_guru['id'];
        $nama_guru = (string) $data_guru['nama'];
    } else {
        $guru_error = true;
    }
} else {
    $guru_error = true;
    error_log('SIPN: Gagal menyiapkan query identitas guru: ' . $conn->error);
}

// Semua aksi POST diarahkan kembali ke halaman ini agar flash message tampil satu kali.
$redirect_to_page = static function () {
    header('Location: ' . BASE_URL . 'guru/data_nilai_siswa.php');
    exit;
};

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $posted_token = (string) ($_POST['csrf_token'] ?? '');

    if (!hash_equals($csrf_token, $posted_token)) {
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Sesi formulir tidak valid atau telah kedaluwarsa. Silakan coba kembali.');
        $redirect_to_page();
    }

    if ($id_guru <= 0) {
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Profil guru tidak ditemukan. Hubungi administrator sekolah.');
        $redirect_to_page();
    }

    // Simpan atau perbarui nilai rapor.
    if (isset($_POST['simpan_nilai'])) {
        $siswa_id = filter_var($_POST['siswa_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        $mapel_id = filter_var($_POST['mapel_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        $nilai_kkm = filter_var($_POST['nilai_kkm'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        $nilai_pengetahuan = filter_var($_POST['nilai_pengetahuan'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        $nilai_keterampilan = filter_var($_POST['nilai_keterampilan'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);

        $nilai_valid = (
            $siswa_id !== false && $siswa_id > 0 &&
            $mapel_id !== false && $mapel_id > 0 &&
            $nilai_kkm !== false && $nilai_kkm >= 0 && $nilai_kkm <= 100 &&
            $nilai_pengetahuan !== false && $nilai_pengetahuan >= 0 && $nilai_pengetahuan <= 100 &&
            $nilai_keterampilan !== false && $nilai_keterampilan >= 0 && $nilai_keterampilan <= 100
        );

        if (!$nilai_valid) {
            set_flash_message('warning', 'Periksa kembali pilihan siswa, mata pelajaran, KKM, dan nilai. Semua nilai harus berada pada rentang 0–100.');
            $redirect_to_page();
        }

        // Validasi kepemilikan mapel dilakukan di server, bukan hanya mengandalkan isi dropdown.
        $stmt_mapel = $conn->prepare('SELECT id FROM mapel WHERE id = ? AND guru_id = ? LIMIT 1');
        $mapel_milik_guru = false;
        if ($stmt_mapel) {
            $stmt_mapel->bind_param('ii', $mapel_id, $id_guru);
            $stmt_mapel->execute();
            $result_mapel = $stmt_mapel->get_result();
            $mapel_milik_guru = $result_mapel && $result_mapel->num_rows > 0;
            $stmt_mapel->close();
        }

        // Pastikan siswa yang dipilih benar-benar terdaftar.
        $stmt_siswa = $conn->prepare('SELECT id FROM siswa WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1');
        $siswa_terdaftar = false;
        if ($stmt_siswa) {
            $stmt_siswa->bind_param('i', $siswa_id);
            $stmt_siswa->execute();
            $result_siswa = $stmt_siswa->get_result();
            $siswa_terdaftar = $result_siswa && $result_siswa->num_rows > 0;
            $stmt_siswa->close();
        }

        if (!$mapel_milik_guru || !$siswa_terdaftar) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Mata pelajaran atau siswa tidak valid untuk akun guru ini.');
            $redirect_to_page();
        }

        $rata_rata = ($nilai_pengetahuan + $nilai_keterampilan) / 2;
        $keterangan = ($rata_rata >= $nilai_kkm) ? 'Tuntas' : 'Belum Tuntas';

        // Pertahankan mekanisme upsert yang digunakan sistem sebelumnya.
        $stmt_cek = $conn->prepare('SELECT id FROM nilai_rapor WHERE siswa_id = ? AND mapel_id = ? LIMIT 1');
        if (!$stmt_cek) {
            error_log('SIPN: Gagal memeriksa entri nilai sebelumnya: ' . $conn->error);
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Data nilai belum dapat diperiksa. Silakan coba kembali.');
            $redirect_to_page();
        }

        $stmt_cek->bind_param('ii', $siswa_id, $mapel_id);
        $stmt_cek->execute();
        $result_cek = $stmt_cek->get_result();
        $nilai_sudah_ada = $result_cek && $result_cek->num_rows > 0;
        $stmt_cek->close();

        if ($nilai_sudah_ada) {
            $stmt_simpan = $conn->prepare(
                'UPDATE nilai_rapor
                 SET nilai_kkm = ?, nilai_pengetahuan = ?, nilai_keterampilan = ?, Keterangan = ?
                 WHERE siswa_id = ? AND mapel_id = ?'
            );
            if ($stmt_simpan) {
                $stmt_simpan->bind_param(
                    'iiisii',
                    $nilai_kkm,
                    $nilai_pengetahuan,
                    $nilai_keterampilan,
                    $keterangan,
                    $siswa_id,
                    $mapel_id
                );
            }
        } else {
            $stmt_simpan = $conn->prepare(
                'INSERT INTO nilai_rapor
                 (siswa_id, mapel_id, nilai_kkm, nilai_pengetahuan, nilai_keterampilan, Keterangan)
                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)'
            );
            if ($stmt_simpan) {
                $stmt_simpan->bind_param(
                    'iiiiis',
                    $siswa_id,
                    $mapel_id,
                    $nilai_kkm,
                    $nilai_pengetahuan,
                    $nilai_keterampilan,
                    $keterangan
                );
            }
        }

        if (isset($stmt_simpan) && $stmt_simpan && $stmt_simpan->execute()) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data nilai rapor berhasil disimpan.');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menyimpan data nilai. Silakan periksa koneksi database.');
            error_log('SIPN: Gagal menyimpan nilai rapor. ' . $conn->error);
        }

        if (isset($stmt_simpan) && $stmt_simpan) {
            $stmt_simpan->close();
        }

        $redirect_to_page();
    }

    // Hapus nilai hanya jika data tersebut berada pada mata pelajaran milik guru aktif.
    if (isset($_POST['aksi']) && $_POST['aksi'] === 'hapus_nilai') {
        $id_nilai = filter_var($_POST['id_nilai'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);

        if ($id_nilai === false || $id_nilai <= 0) {
            set_flash_message('warning', 'Data nilai yang dipilih tidak valid.');
            $redirect_to_page();
        }

        $stmt_hapus = $conn->prepare(
            'DELETE nr
             FROM nilai_rapor nr
             INNER JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
             WHERE nr.id = ? AND m.guru_id = ?'
        );

        if ($stmt_hapus) {
            $stmt_hapus->bind_param('ii', $id_nilai, $id_guru);
            $hapus_berhasil = $stmt_hapus->execute() && $stmt_hapus->affected_rows > 0;
            $stmt_hapus->close();

            if ($hapus_berhasil) {
                set_flash_message('success', 'Data nilai rapor berhasil dihapus.');
            } else {
                set_flash_message('warning', 'Data tidak ditemukan atau bukan bagian dari mata pelajaran Anda.');
            }
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menyiapkan proses penghapusan data.');
            error_log('SIPN: Gagal menyiapkan hapus nilai: ' . $conn->error);
        }

        $redirect_to_page();
    }
}

// Ambil mata pelajaran yang dimiliki guru aktif.
$mapel_list = [];
if ($id_guru > 0) {
    $stmt_mapel_list = $conn->prepare(
        'SELECT id, kode_mapel, nama_mapel
         FROM mapel
         WHERE guru_id = ?
         ORDER BY nama_mapel ASC'
    );

    if ($stmt_mapel_list) {
        $stmt_mapel_list->bind_param('i', $id_guru);
        $stmt_mapel_list->execute();
        $result_mapel_list = $stmt_mapel_list->get_result();
        while ($result_mapel_list && ($mapel = $result_mapel_list->fetch_assoc())) {
            $mapel_list[] = $mapel;
        }
        $stmt_mapel_list->close();
    }
}

// Daftar siswa dipertahankan sesuai alur halaman sebelumnya.
$siswa_list = [];
$stmt_siswa_list = $conn->prepare('SELECT id, nis, nama, kelas FROM siswa ORDER BY nama ASC');
if ($stmt_siswa_list) {
    $stmt_siswa_list->execute();
    $result_siswa_list = $stmt_siswa_list->get_result();
    while ($result_siswa_list && ($siswa = $result_siswa_list->fetch_assoc())) {
        $siswa_list[] = $siswa;
    }
    $stmt_siswa_list->close();
}

// Tampilkan hanya nilai yang berada pada mata pelajaran milik guru yang sedang login.
$nilai_list = [];
$stmt_nilai = $conn->prepare(
    'SELECT
        nr.id,
        nr.siswa_id,
        nr.mapel_id,
        nr.nilai_kkm,
        nr.nilai_pengetahuan,
        nr.nilai_keterampilan,
        nr.Keterangan,
        s.nama AS nama_siswa,
        s.nis,
        s.kelas,
        m.kode_mapel,
        m.nama_mapel
     FROM nilai_rapor nr
     INNER JOIN siswa s ON nr.siswa_id = s.id
     INNER JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
     WHERE m.guru_id = ?
     ORDER BY s.kelas ASC, s.nama ASC, m.nama_mapel ASC'
);

if ($stmt_nilai) {
    $stmt_nilai->bind_param('i', $id_guru);
    $stmt_nilai->execute();
    $result_nilai = $stmt_nilai->get_result();
    while ($result_nilai && ($nilai = $result_nilai->fetch_assoc())) {
        $nilai_list[] = $nilai;
    }
    $stmt_nilai->close();
} else {
    error_log('SIPN: Gagal menyiapkan daftar nilai guru: ' . $conn->error);
}

$total_nilai = count($nilai_list);
$total_tuntas = 0;
$total_belum_tuntas = 0;

foreach ($nilai_list as $nilai) {
    if (strtolower(trim((string) $nilai['Keterangan'])) === 'tuntas') {
        $total_tuntas++;
    } else {
        $total_belum_tuntas++;
    }
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/data_nilai_siswa_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

</file>

                                        <span class="sipn-grade-student__details">
                                            <strong><?= $escape($row['nama_siswa']); ?></strong>
                                            <small>NIS <?= $escape($row['nis']); ?></small>
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                                <td><span class="sipn-grade-class"><?= $escape($row['kelas']); ?></span></td>
                                <td>
                                    <span class="sipn-grade-subject">
                                        <strong><?= $escape($row['nama_mapel']); ?></strong>
                                        <small><?= $escape($row['kode_mapel']); ?></small>
                                    </span>
                                </td>
                                <td class="is-center"><span class="sipn-grade-score sipn-grade-score--muted"><?= (int) $row['nilai_kkm']; ?></span></td>
                                <td class="is-center"><strong class="sipn-grade-score-value"><?= $nilai_pengetahuan; ?></strong></td>
                                <td class="is-center"><strong class="sipn-grade-score-value"><?= $nilai_keterampilan; ?></strong></td>
                                <td class="is-center"><span class="sipn-grade-average"><?= number_format($rata_rata_row, 1, ',', '.'); ?></span></td>
                                <td><span class="sipn-grade-status <?= $status_class; ?>"><i class="bi <?= $status_normal === 'tuntas' ? 'bi-check-circle-fill' : 'bi-exclamation-circle-fill'; ?>" aria-hidden="true"></i><?= $escape($status_label); ?></span></td>
                                <td class="is-center">
                                    <div class="sipn-grade-actions">
                                        <button type="button" class="sipn-grade-icon-button sipn-grade-icon-button--edit" data-modal-target="#modalEditNilai<?= (int) $row['id']; ?>" aria-label="Edit nilai <?= $escape($row['nama_siswa']); ?>" title="Edit nilai">
                                            <i class="bi bi-pencil-square" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                                        </button>
                                        <button type="button" class="sipn-grade-icon-button sipn-grade-icon-button--delete" data-modal-target="#modalHapusNilai<?= (int) $row['id']; ?>" aria-label="Hapus nilai <?= $escape($row['nama_siswa']); ?>" title="Hapus nilai">
                                            <i class="bi bi-trash3" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        <?php endforeach; ?>
                        <tr id="gradeNoResults" hidden>
                            <td colspan="10">
                                <div class="sipn-grade-no-results">
                                    <i class="bi bi-search" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                                    <strong>Data tidak ditemukan</strong>
                                    <span>Coba ubah kata kunci atau filter yang digunakan.</span>
                                </div>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    <?php else: ?>
                        <tr>
                            <td colspan="10">
                                <div class="sipn-grade-empty">
                                    <span class="sipn-grade-empty__icon"><i class="bi bi-journal-x" aria-hidden="true"></i></span>
                                    <strong>Belum ada nilai rapor</strong>
                                    <p>Belum ada nilai yang tercatat untuk mata pelajaran yang Anda ampu. Gunakan tombol “Input Nilai Baru” untuk mulai mengisi nilai.</p>
                                </div>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    <?php endif; ?>
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
        <div class="sipn-grade-panel__footer">
            <span><i class="bi bi-shield-check" aria-hidden="true"></i> Data dibatasi berdasarkan mata pelajaran milik akun guru aktif.</span>
            <span>Terakhir dimuat: <?= date('d/m/Y H:i'); ?></span>
        </div>
    </section>
</div>

<!-- Modal input nilai baru -->
<div class="modal-overlay sipn-grade-modal" id="modalTambahNilai" aria-hidden="true">
    <div class="modal-box modal-lg">
        <form method="POST" autocomplete="off">
            <div class="modal-header header-primary">
                <div>
                    <span class="sipn-grade-modal-kicker">Entri akademik</span>

<file path="guru/dashboard_guru.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';

check_role(['guru']);

$id_user = (int) ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);

$query_guru = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM guru WHERE user_id = '$id_user'");
$guru = $query_guru ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_guru) : null;
$id_guru = (int) ($guru['id'] ?? 0);
$nama_guru = $guru['nama'] ?? ($_SESSION['username'] ?? 'Guru');
$nip = $guru['nip'] ?? '-';

$q_mapel = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM mapel WHERE guru_id = '$id_guru'");
$row_mapel = $q_mapel ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_mapel) : null;
$total_mapel = $q_mapel ? (int) ($row_mapel['total'] ?? 0) : null;

$q_siswa = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM siswa");
$row_siswa = $q_siswa ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_siswa) : null;
$total_siswa = $q_siswa ? (int) ($row_siswa['total'] ?? 0) : null;

$q_nilai_input = mysqli_query($conn, "
    SELECT COUNT(nr.id) AS total
    FROM nilai_rapor nr
    JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
    WHERE m.guru_id = '$id_guru'
");
$row_nilai = $q_nilai_input ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_nilai_input) : null;
$total_nilai_input = $q_nilai_input ? (int) ($row_nilai['total'] ?? 0) : null;

$query_mapel_list = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, kode_mapel, nama_mapel FROM mapel WHERE guru_id = '$id_guru' ORDER BY nama_mapel ASC");

$dashboard_query_error = !$query_guru || !$q_mapel || !$q_siswa || !$q_nilai_input || !$query_mapel_list;

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/dashboard_guru_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

</file>

                                <th class="sipn-rapor-table__center">Pengetahuan</th>
                                <th class="sipn-rapor-table__center">Keterampilan</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            <?php
                            $nomor = 1;
                            $urutan_baris = 0;
                            while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_nilai)):
                                $keterangan = trim((string) ($row['Keterangan'] ?? '-'));
                                $is_tuntas = strtolower($keterangan) === 'tuntas';
                                $delay_baris = min($urutan_baris * 65, 780);
                            ?>
                                <tr class="sipn-rapor-row" style="--row-delay: <?= $delay_baris; ?>ms;">
                                    <td class="sipn-rapor-table__number"><?= $nomor++; ?></td>
                                    <td class="sipn-rapor-subject-cell">
                                        <strong><?= htmlspecialchars((string) $row['nama_mapel'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?></strong>
                                        <span><?= htmlspecialchars((string) $row['kode_mapel'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?></span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="sipn-rapor-teacher-cell"><?= htmlspecialchars((string) $row['nama_guru'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?></td>
                                    <td class="sipn-rapor-table__center"><span class="sipn-rapor-score sipn-rapor-score--target"><?= htmlspecialchars((string) $row['nilai_kkm'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?></span></td>
                                    <td class="sipn-rapor-table__center"><span class="sipn-rapor-score"><?= htmlspecialchars((string) $row['nilai_pengetahuan'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?></span></td>
                                    <td class="sipn-rapor-table__center"><span class="sipn-rapor-score"><?= htmlspecialchars((string) $row['nilai_keterampilan'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?></span></td>
                                    <td class="sipn-rapor-table__center">
                                        <span class="sipn-rapor-status <?= $is_tuntas ? 'sipn-rapor-status--success' : 'sipn-rapor-status--other'; ?>">
                                            <i class="bi <?= $is_tuntas ? 'bi-check-circle-fill' : 'bi-info-circle-fill'; ?>" aria-hidden="true"></i>
                                            <?= htmlspecialchars($keterangan, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8'); ?>
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            <?php
                                $urutan_baris++;
                            endwhile;
                            ?>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        <?php elseif ($query_nilai): ?>
            <div class="sipn-rapor-empty">
                <span class="sipn-rapor-empty__icon" aria-hidden="true"><i class="bi bi-journal-x"></i></span>
                <strong>Belum ada nilai rapor</strong>
                <p>Nilai akan ditampilkan di halaman ini setelah guru pengampu memasukkannya ke SIPN.</p>
            </div>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <div class="sipn-rapor-signature print-only">
            <p>Cimahi, <?= date('d F Y'); ?><br>Mengetahui,<br>Wali Kelas / Guru Pengampu</p>
            <span>( ........................................................... )</span>
        </div>
    </section>

    <p class="sipn-rapor-footnote no-print">
        <i class="bi bi-shield-check" aria-hidden="true"></i>
        Data nilai ditampilkan sesuai catatan yang tersimpan di sistem SIPN.
    </p>
</div>

<file path="siswa/lihat_nilai_rapor.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';

check_role(['siswa']);

$user_id = (int) ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);

// Ambil identitas siswa dari akun yang sedang aktif.
$query_siswa = mysqli_query(
    $conn,
    "SELECT id, nama, nis, kelas FROM siswa WHERE user_id = '$user_id' LIMIT 1"
);
$siswa = $query_siswa ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_siswa) : null;
$id_siswa = (int) ($siswa['id'] ?? 0);

// Ambil nilai rapor beserta mata pelajaran dan guru pengampu.
$query_nilai = mysqli_query($conn, "
    SELECT
        nr.nilai_kkm,
        nr.nilai_pengetahuan,
        nr.nilai_keterampilan,
        nr.Keterangan,
        m.kode_mapel,
        m.nama_mapel,
        g.nama AS nama_guru
    FROM nilai_rapor nr
    JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
    JOIN guru g ON m.guru_id = g.id
    WHERE nr.siswa_id = '$id_siswa'
    ORDER BY m.nama_mapel ASC
");

$total_mapel = $query_nilai ? mysqli_num_rows($query_nilai) : 0;

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/lihat_nilai_rapor_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

</file>

<file path="siswa/dashboard_siswa.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';

check_role(['siswa']);

$user_id = (int) ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);

// Ambil data siswa berdasarkan akun yang sedang login.
$query_siswa = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM siswa WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
$siswa = $query_siswa ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_siswa) : null;

if (!$siswa) {
    die("Data profil siswa tidak ditemukan. Hubungi Administrator.");
}

$id_siswa   = (int) $siswa['id'];
$nama_siswa = $siswa['nama'];
$nis        = $siswa['nis'];
$kelas      = $siswa['kelas'];

// Hitung statistik berdasarkan data akademik siswa.
$query_stat = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_nilai FROM nilai_rapor WHERE siswa_id = '$id_siswa'");
$stat = $query_stat ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_stat) : null;
$total_nilai = $query_stat ? (int) ($stat['total_nilai'] ?? 0) : null;

$query_mapel = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_mapel FROM mengajar WHERE siswa_id = '$id_siswa'");
$stat_mapel = $query_mapel ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_mapel) : null;
$total_mapel = $query_mapel ? (int) ($stat_mapel['total_mapel'] ?? 0) : null;

// Ambil daftar nilai rapor untuk ditampilkan pada dashboard.
$query_nilai_list = mysqli_query($conn, "
    SELECT nr.*, m.kode_mapel, m.nama_mapel, g.nama AS nama_guru
    FROM nilai_rapor nr
    JOIN mapel m ON nr.mapel_id = m.id
    JOIN guru g ON m.guru_id = g.id
    WHERE nr.siswa_id = '$id_siswa'
    ORDER BY m.nama_mapel ASC
");

$dashboard_query_error = !$query_siswa || !$query_stat || !$query_mapel || !$query_nilai_list;

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/dashboard_siswa_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

<file path="profile.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/includes/helper_cek_login.php';

if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$user_id = $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role = $_SESSION['role'];

// 1. Ambil Data Gabungan (Users + Guru/Siswa)
$query_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT username, role, foto FROM users WHERE id = '$user_id'");
$data_user = mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_user);

$nama_lengkap = $data_user['username'];
$identitas_id = '';
$jenis_kelamin = '';
$kelas = '';

if ($role === 'guru') {
    $q_detail = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT nip, nama, jenis_kelamin FROM guru WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
    if ($d = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_detail)) {
        $nama_lengkap = $d['nama'];
        $identitas_id = $d['nip'];
        $jenis_kelamin = $d['jenis_kelamin'];
    }
} elseif ($role === 'siswa') {
    $q_detail = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT nis, nama, kelas, jenis_kelamin FROM siswa WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
    if ($d = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_detail)) {
        $nama_lengkap = $d['nama'];
        $identitas_id = $d['nis'];
        $kelas = $d['kelas'];
        $jenis_kelamin = $d['jenis_kelamin'];
    }
} elseif ($role === 'admin') {
    $nama_lengkap = 'Administrator Sistem';
}

// 2. Proses Update Informasi Menyeluruh
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['update_informasi'])) {
    $username_baru = sanitize($_POST['username']);
    $password_baru = trim($_POST['password']);
    
    mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
    try {
        // A. Update tabel users
        $q_user_update = "UPDATE users SET username = '$username_baru'";
        if (!empty($password_baru)) {
            $password_hash = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
            $q_user_update .= ", password = '$password_hash'";
        }
        $q_user_update .= " WHERE id = '$user_id'";
        mysqli_query($conn, $q_user_update);
        
        // B. Update tabel spesifik sesuai role
        if ($role === 'guru') {
            $nip = sanitize($_POST['nip']);
            $nama = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
            $jk = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE guru SET nip = '$nip', nama = '$nama', jenis_kelamin = '$jk' WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
        } elseif ($role === 'siswa') {
            $nis = sanitize($_POST['nis']);
            $nama = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
            $kls = sanitize($_POST['kelas']);
            $jk = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE siswa SET nis = '$nis', nama = '$nama', kelas = '$kls', jenis_kelamin = '$jk' WHERE user_id = '$user_id'");
        }

        mysqli_commit($conn);
        $_SESSION['username'] = $username_baru; // Update session
        set_flash_message('success', 'Informasi profil berhasil diperbarui secara menyeluruh!');
    } catch (Exception $e) {
        mysqli_rollback($conn);
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui profil: ' . $e->getMessage());
    }
    
    header("Location: profile.php");
    exit;
}

// 3. Proses Update Foto (Upload Asli ke Direktori & Database)
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['update_foto'])) {
    if (isset($_FILES['foto_profil']) && $_FILES['foto_profil']['error'] === 0) {
        $file = $_FILES['foto_profil'];
        $nama_file = $file['name'];
        $ukuran_file = $file['size'];
        $tmp_name = $file['tmp_name'];
        
        $ekstensi_diperbolehkan = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png'];
        $ekstensi = strtolower(pathinfo($nama_file, PATHINFO_EXTENSION));
        
        // Cek ekstensi file
        if (in_array($ekstensi, $ekstensi_diperbolehkan)) {
            // Cek ukuran file (Maksimal 2MB = 2097152 bytes)
            if ($ukuran_file <= 2097152) {
                // Buat nama file unik agar tidak bentrok
                $nama_file_baru = 'profil_' . $role . '_' . $user_id . '_' . time() . '.' . $ekstensi;
                
                // Tentukan lokasi folder penyimpanan (Otomatis dibuat jika belum ada)
                $direktori = __DIR__ . '/assets/img/profil/';
                if (!is_dir($direktori)) {
                    mkdir($direktori, 0777, true);
                }
                
                $path_simpan = $direktori . $nama_file_baru;
                
                // Pindahkan file dari memori sementara ke folder tujuan
                if (move_uploaded_file($tmp_name, $path_simpan)) {
                    
                    // (Opsional & Aman): Hapus foto lama di folder agar server tidak bengkak
                    if (!empty($data_user['foto']) && file_exists($direktori . $data_user['foto'])) {
                        unlink($direktori . $data_user['foto']);
                    }
                    
                    // Simpan nama file baru ke database
                    $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE users SET foto = ? WHERE id = ?");
                    mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "si", $nama_file_baru, $user_id);
                    
                    if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
                        set_flash_message('success', 'Foto profil berhasil diperbarui!');
                    } else {
                        set_flash_message('danger', 'File terunggah, tapi gagal menyimpan ke database.');
                    }
                } else {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Sistem gagal memindahkan file foto ke direktori server.');
                }
            } else {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Ukuran foto terlalu besar (Maksimal 2MB).');
            }
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Format file tidak didukung. Gunakan JPG atau PNG.');
        }
    } else {
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Terjadi kesalahan atau tidak ada file yang dipilih.');
    }
    
    header("Location: profile.php");
    exit;
}
require_once __DIR__ . '/includes/template_header.php';
?>

<div class="profile-container-centered">
    
    <!-- Profil Card Berada di Tengah -->
    <div class="profile-card-centered">

        <div class="avatar-circle-centered" style="overflow: hidden; padding: 0;">
    <?php if (!empty($data_user['foto'])): ?>
        <img src="<?= BASE_URL; ?>assets/img/profil/<?= htmlspecialchars($data_user['foto']); ?>" alt="Foto Profil" style="width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover;">
    <?php else: ?>
        <i class="bi bi-person"></i>
    <?php endif; ?>
        </div>
            
            <h2 class="profile-name"><?= htmlspecialchars($nama_lengkap); ?></h2>
            <div class="profile-role-badge">
                <i class="bi bi-shield-check"></i> Hak Akses: <?= ucfirst($role); ?>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Bagian Bawah: Informasi Detail -->

                <div class="info-label"><i class="bi bi-person-badge"></i> Username Akun</div>
                <div class="info-value"><?= htmlspecialchars($data_user['username']); ?></div>
            </div>

            <?php if ($role === 'guru'): ?>
                <div class="info-row">
                    <div class="info-label"><i class="bi bi-hash"></i> NIP Guru</div>
                    <div class="info-value" style="font-family: monospace; font-size: 1.15rem;"><?= htmlspecialchars($identitas_id); ?></div>
                </div>
                <div class="info-row">
                    <div class="info-label"><i class="bi bi-gender-ambiguous"></i> Jenis Kelamin</div>
                    <div class="info-value"><?= $jenis_kelamin === 'L' ? 'Laki-laki' : 'Perempuan'; ?></div>
                </div>
            <?php endif; ?>

            <?php if ($role === 'siswa'): ?>
                <div class="info-row">
                    <div class="info-label"><i class="bi bi-hash"></i> NIS Siswa</div>
                    <div class="info-value" style="font-family: monospace; font-size: 1.15rem;"><?= htmlspecialchars($identitas_id); ?></div>
                </div>
                <div class="info-row">
                    <div class="info-label"><i class="bi bi-bookmark-star"></i> Kelas / Rombel</div>
                    <div class="info-value"><?= htmlspecialchars($kelas); ?></div>
                </div>
                <div class="info-row">
                    <div class="info-label"><i class="bi bi-gender-ambiguous"></i> Jenis Kelamin</div>
                    <div class="info-value"><?= $jenis_kelamin === 'L' ? 'Laki-laki' : 'Perempuan'; ?></div>
                </div>
            <?php endif; ?>
            
            <div class="info-row">
                <div class="info-label"><i class="bi bi-shield-lock"></i> Keamanan Sandi</div>
                <div class="info-value" style="color: var(--text-muted);">•••••••• (Terenkripsi)</div>
            </div>

<!-- SCRIPT LIVE PREVIEW FOTO -->
<script>
function previewFoto(input) {
    const kontenAwal = document.getElementById('dropzoneContentAwal');
    const wadahPreview = document.getElementById('wadahPreview');
    const gambarPreview = document.getElementById('gambarPreview');
    const badgeFile = document.getElementById('badgeNamaFile');
    const teksNamaFile = document.getElementById('teksNamaFile');

    if (input.files && input.files[0]) {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        
        reader.onload = function(e) {
            // Sembunyikan ikon awan dan tampilkan foto
            kontenAwal.style.display = 'none';
            wadahPreview.style.display = 'block';
            gambarPreview.src = e.target.result;
            
            // Tampilkan badge nama file
            badgeFile.style.display = 'inline-flex';
            teksNamaFile.textContent = input.files[0].name;
        }
        
        reader.readAsDataURL(input.files[0]);
    } else {
        // Kembalikan ke tampilan awal jika pengguna batal memilih file
        kontenAwal.style.display = 'block';
        wadahPreview.style.display = 'none';
        badgeFile.style.display = 'none';
        gambarPreview.src = "";
    }
}
</script>

<?php require_once __DIR__ . '/includes/template_footer.php'; ?>
</file>

<?php
// Mencegah redeclaration BASE_URL jika dipanggil berulang
if (!defined('BASE_URL')) {
    define('BASE_URL', 'http://localhost:8000/');
}

$host     = "localhost";
$username = "putra";
$password = "password123";
$database = "db_siacad_smk_2";

// Membuat Koneksi

// Cek Koneksi
if (!$conn) {

}

<file path="admin/data_mengajar.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['admin']);

$e = static function ($value): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string) ($value ?? ''), ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
};

// Proses penambahan dan penghapusan penugasan tetap menggunakan tabel mengajar.
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = (string) $_POST['aksi'];

    if ($aksi === 'tambah') {
        $mapel_id = (int) ($_POST['mapel_id'] ?? 0);
        $siswa_id = (int) ($_POST['siswa_id'] ?? 0);

        if ($mapel_id <= 0 || $siswa_id <= 0) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Pilih mata pelajaran dan siswa yang valid.');
        } else {
            // Pastikan kedua data induk tersedia sebelum membuat relasi.
            $stmt_validasi = mysqli_prepare(
                $conn,
                'SELECT m.id FROM mapel m CROSS JOIN siswa s WHERE m.id = ? AND s.id = ? LIMIT 1'
            );

            if (!$stmt_validasi) {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Validasi penugasan tidak dapat dijalankan.');
            } else {
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt_validasi, 'ii', $mapel_id, $siswa_id);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt_validasi);
                $validasi_result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt_validasi);
                $data_valid = $validasi_result && mysqli_num_rows($validasi_result) === 1;
                mysqli_stmt_close($stmt_validasi);

                if (!$data_valid) {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Mata pelajaran atau siswa yang dipilih tidak ditemukan.');
                } else {
                    // Cegah relasi siswa-mapel ganda.
                    $stmt_cek = mysqli_prepare(
                        $conn,
                        'SELECT id FROM mengajar WHERE mapel_id = ? AND siswa_id = ? LIMIT 1'
                    );

                    if (!$stmt_cek) {
                        set_flash_message('danger', 'Pemeriksaan penugasan gagal dijalankan.');
                    } else {
                        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt_cek, 'ii', $mapel_id, $siswa_id);
                        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt_cek);
                        $cek_result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt_cek);
                        $sudah_ada = $cek_result && mysqli_num_rows($cek_result) > 0;
                        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt_cek);

                        if ($sudah_ada) {
                            set_flash_message('warning', 'Siswa tersebut sudah terdaftar pada mata pelajaran ini.');
                        } else {
                            $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'INSERT INTO mengajar (mapel_id, siswa_id) VALUES (?, ?)');
                            if ($stmt) {
                                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'ii', $mapel_id, $siswa_id);
                                if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
                                    set_flash_message('success', 'Penugasan siswa berhasil ditambahkan!');
                                } else {
                                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menyimpan penugasan siswa.');
                                }
                                mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
                            } else {
                                set_flash_message('danger', 'Penugasan tidak dapat disimpan saat ini.');
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    } elseif ($aksi === 'hapus') {
        $id_mengajar = (int) ($_POST['id_mengajar'] ?? 0);

        if ($id_mengajar <= 0) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Penugasan yang dipilih tidak valid.');
        } else {
            $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'DELETE FROM mengajar WHERE id = ?');
            if ($stmt) {
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'i', $id_mengajar);
                if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt) && mysqli_stmt_affected_rows($stmt) > 0) {
                    set_flash_message('success', 'Penugasan berhasil dihapus!');
                } else {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus penugasan. Data mungkin sudah tidak tersedia.');
                }
                mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
            } else {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Permintaan penghapusan tidak dapat diproses.');
            }
        }
    } else {
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Aksi penugasan tidak dikenali.');
    }

    header('Location: data_mengajar.php');
    exit();
}

// Daftar penugasan beserta identitas mapel, guru, dan siswa.
$query = "
    SELECT
        mg.id,
        mg.mapel_id,
        mg.siswa_id,
        m.nama_mapel,
        m.kode_mapel,
        g.nama AS nama_guru,
        s.nama AS nama_siswa,
        s.nis,
        s.kelas
    FROM mengajar mg
    JOIN mapel m ON mg.mapel_id = m.id
    JOIN guru g ON m.guru_id = g.id
    JOIN siswa s ON mg.siswa_id = s.id
    ORDER BY m.nama_mapel ASC, s.kelas ASC, s.nama ASC
";
$result_penugasan = mysqli_query($conn, $query);
$penugasan = [];
if ($result_penugasan) {
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result_penugasan)) {
        $penugasan[] = $row;
    }
}

// Opsi modal disusun sebagai array agar nilai dari database selalu di-escape saat dirender.
$mapel_list = [];
$q_mapel = mysqli_query(
    $conn,
    'SELECT m.id, m.kode_mapel, m.nama_mapel, g.nama AS nama_guru FROM mapel m LEFT JOIN guru g ON m.guru_id = g.id ORDER BY m.nama_mapel ASC'
);
if ($q_mapel) {
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_mapel)) {
        $mapel_list[] = $row;
    }
}

$siswa_list = [];
$q_siswa = mysqli_query($conn, 'SELECT id, nis, nama, kelas FROM siswa ORDER BY kelas ASC, nama ASC');
if ($q_siswa) {
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_siswa)) {
        $siswa_list[] = $row;
    }
}

$total_penugasan = count($penugasan);
$unique_mapel = [];
$unique_siswa = [];
foreach ($penugasan as $row) {
    $unique_mapel[(int) $row['mapel_id']] = true;
    $unique_siswa[(int) $row['siswa_id']] = true;
}
$jumlah_mapel_terpakai = count($unique_mapel);
$jumlah_siswa_terdaftar = count($unique_siswa);
$query_error = !$result_penugasan || !$q_mapel || !$q_siswa;

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/views/data_mengajar_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

</file>

<file path="admin/data_siswa.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['admin']);

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = $_POST['aksi'];

    if ($aksi === 'tambah') {
        $nis      = sanitize($_POST['nis']);
        $nama     = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
        $kelas    = sanitize($_POST['kelas']);
        $jk       = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
        $username = sanitize($_POST['username']);
        $password = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        $role     = 'siswa';

        $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username='$username' UNION SELECT id FROM siswa WHERE nis='$nis'");
        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Username atau NIS sudah terdaftar.');
        } else {
            mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
            try {
                $stmt1 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)");
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt1, "sss", $username, $password, $role);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt1);
                $user_id = mysqli_insert_id($conn);

                $stmt2 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO siswa (nis, nama, kelas, jenis_kelamin, user_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)");
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt2, "ssssi", $nis, $nama, $kelas, $jk, $user_id);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt2);

                mysqli_commit($conn);
                set_flash_message('success', 'Data Siswa dan Akun berhasil ditambahkan!');
            } catch (Exception $e) {
                mysqli_rollback($conn);
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Terjadi kesalahan sistem: ' . $e->getMessage());
            }
        }
    } 
    elseif ($aksi === 'edit') {
        $id_siswa = (int)$_POST['id_siswa'];
        $nis      = sanitize($_POST['nis']);
        $nama     = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
        $kelas    = sanitize($_POST['kelas']);
        $jk       = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE siswa SET nis=?, nama=?, kelas=?, jenis_kelamin=? WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "ssssi", $nis, $nama, $kelas, $jk, $id_siswa);
        
        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data Siswa berhasil diperbarui!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui data Siswa.');
        }
    }
    elseif ($aksi === 'hapus') {
        $user_id = (int)$_POST['user_id'];
        
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $user_id);
        
        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data Siswa beserta akun berhasil dihapus!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus data.');
        }
    }
    header("Location: data_siswa.php");
    exit();
}

$query = "SELECT s.*, u.username FROM siswa s JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.id ORDER BY s.kelas ASC, s.nama ASC";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);
$students = [];
if ($result) {
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
        $students[] = $row;
    }
}

$total_siswa = count($students);
$total_kelas = count(array_unique(array_map(static fn($row) => (string) ($row['kelas'] ?? ''), $students)));
$total_siswa_laki = count(array_filter($students, static fn($row) => ($row['jenis_kelamin'] ?? '') === 'L'));
$total_siswa_perempuan = count(array_filter($students, static fn($row) => ($row['jenis_kelamin'] ?? '') === 'P'));
$data_siswa_error = !$result;

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/views/data_siswa_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

</file>

                        </td>
                        <td><span class="sipn-admin-data-pill is-violet"><?= $e($row['kelas']); ?></span></td>
                        <td class="sipn-admin-data-actions" data-search-ignore>
                            <form method="POST" class="sipn-admin-inline-delete" onsubmit="return confirm('Hapus penugasan <?= $e($row['nama_siswa']); ?> pada mata pelajaran <?= $e($row['nama_mapel']); ?>?');">
                                <input type="hidden" name="aksi" value="hapus">
                                <input type="hidden" name="id_mengajar" value="<?= (int) $row['id']; ?>">

                            </form>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                <?php else: ?>
                    <tr><td colspan="6" class="sipn-admin-data-empty"><i class="bi bi-diagram-3" aria-hidden="true"></i><strong>Belum ada penugasan</strong><span>Penugasan siswa ke mata pelajaran akan tampil di sini.</span></td></tr>
                <?php endif; ?>
                </tbody>
            </table>
            <div class="sipn-admin-data-no-match" data-admin-search-empty hidden><i class="bi bi-search" aria-hidden="true"></i><strong>Data tidak ditemukan</strong><span>Coba kata kunci lain atau tekan tombol reset.</span></div>

    </section>
