<file path="admin/data_guru.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['admin']);

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = $_POST['aksi'];

    if ($aksi === 'tambah') {
        $nip      = sanitize($_POST['nip']);
        $nama     = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
        $jk       = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
        $username = sanitize($_POST['username']);
        $password = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        $role     = 'guru';

        $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username='$username' UNION SELECT id FROM guru WHERE nip='$nip'");
        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Username atau NIP sudah terdaftar.');
        } else {
            mysqli_begin_transaction($conn);
            try {
                $stmt1 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, role) VALUES (?, ?, ?)");
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt1, "sss", $username, $password, $role);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt1);
                $user_id = mysqli_insert_id($conn);

                $stmt2 = mysqli_prepare($conn, "INSERT INTO guru (nip, nama, jenis_kelamin, user_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)");
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt2, "sssi", $nip, $nama, $jk, $user_id);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt2);

                mysqli_commit($conn);
                set_flash_message('success', 'Data Guru dan Akun berhasil ditambahkan!');
            } catch (Exception $e) {
                mysqli_rollback($conn);
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Terjadi kesalahan sistem: ' . $e->getMessage());
            }
        }
    } 
    elseif ($aksi === 'edit') {
        $id_guru = (int)$_POST['id_guru'];
        $nip     = sanitize($_POST['nip']);
        $nama    = sanitize($_POST['nama']);
        $jk      = sanitize($_POST['jenis_kelamin']);

        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "UPDATE guru SET nip=?, nama=?, jenis_kelamin=? WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "sssi", $nip, $nama, $jk, $id_guru);
        
        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data Guru berhasil diperbarui!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui data Guru.');
        }
    }
    elseif ($aksi === 'hapus') {
        $user_id = (int)$_POST['user_id'];
        
        $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id=?");
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, "i", $user_id);
        
        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
            set_flash_message('success', 'Data Guru beserta akun berhasil dihapus!');
        } else {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus data.');
        }
    }

    header("Location: data_guru.php");
    exit();
}

$query = "SELECT g.*, u.username FROM guru g JOIN users u ON g.user_id = u.id ORDER BY g.nama ASC";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);
$teachers = [];
if ($result) {
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
        $teachers[] = $row;
    }
}

$total_guru = count($teachers);
$total_guru_laki = count(array_filter($teachers, static fn($row) => ($row['jenis_kelamin'] ?? '') === 'L'));
$total_guru_perempuan = count(array_filter($teachers, static fn($row) => ($row['jenis_kelamin'] ?? '') === 'P'));
$data_guru_error = !$result;

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/views/data_guru_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

<file path="admin/data_user.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['admin']);

$logged_in_user_id = (int) ($_SESSION['user_id'] ?? 0);
$allowed_roles = ['admin', 'guru', 'siswa'];

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = (string) $_POST['aksi'];
    $id_user = (int) ($_POST['id_user'] ?? 0);

    if ($aksi === 'reset_password') {
        $password_baru = (string) ($_POST['password_baru'] ?? '');

        if ($id_user <= 0 || strlen($password_baru) < 4) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Reset password gagal. Pastikan akun valid dan password minimal 4 karakter.');
        } else {
            $pass_baru = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
            $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'UPDATE users SET password = ? WHERE id = ?');

            if ($stmt) {
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'si', $pass_baru, $id_user);
                if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt) && mysqli_stmt_affected_rows($stmt) > 0) {
                    set_flash_message('success', 'Password pengguna berhasil diperbarui!');
                } else {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui password.');
                }
                mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
            } else {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Permintaan reset password tidak dapat diproses.');
            }
        }
    } elseif ($aksi === 'edit_user') {
        $username = trim((string) ($_POST['username'] ?? ''));
        $role = (string) ($_POST['role'] ?? '');

        if ($id_user <= 0 || $username === '' || !in_array($role, $allowed_roles, true)) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Data akun tidak valid. Periksa username dan role yang dipilih.');
        } else {
            $stmt_cek = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ? AND id != ? LIMIT 1');

            if (!$stmt_cek) {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Pemeriksaan username gagal dijalankan.');
            } else {
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt_cek, 'si', $username, $id_user);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt_cek);
                $res_cek = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt_cek);
                $username_dipakai = $res_cek && mysqli_num_rows($res_cek) > 0;
                mysqli_stmt_close($stmt_cek);

                if ($username_dipakai) {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Username tersebut sudah digunakan oleh akun lain.');
                } else {
                    $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'UPDATE users SET username = ?, role = ? WHERE id = ?');
                    if ($stmt) {
                        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'ssi', $username, $role, $id_user);
                        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
                            if ($id_user === $logged_in_user_id) {
                                $_SESSION['username'] = $username;
                                $_SESSION['role'] = $role;
                            }
                            set_flash_message('success', 'Data akun user berhasil diperbarui!');
                        } else {
                            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui data user.');
                        }
                        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
                    } else {
                        set_flash_message('danger', 'Data akun tidak dapat diperbarui saat ini.');
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    } elseif ($aksi === 'hapus_user') {
        if ($id_user <= 0) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Akun yang dipilih tidak valid.');
        } elseif ($id_user === $logged_in_user_id) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal! Anda tidak dapat menghapus akun Anda sendiri yang sedang aktif.');
        } else {
            $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'DELETE FROM users WHERE id = ?');
            if ($stmt) {
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'i', $id_user);
                if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt) && mysqli_stmt_affected_rows($stmt) > 0) {
                    set_flash_message('success', 'Akun user berhasil dihapus!');
                } else {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus user. Akun mungkin sudah tidak tersedia atau masih terhubung dengan data lain.');
                }
                mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
            } else {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Permintaan penghapusan akun tidak dapat diproses.');
            }
        }
    }

    header('Location: data_user.php');
    exit();
}

$query = 'SELECT id, username, role FROM users ORDER BY role ASC, username ASC';
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);
$users = [];
if ($result) {
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result)) {
        $users[] = $row;
    }
}

$total_user = count($users);
$total_admin = count(array_filter($users, static fn($row) => ($row['role'] ?? '') === 'admin'));
$total_guru = count(array_filter($users, static fn($row) => ($row['role'] ?? '') === 'guru'));
$total_siswa = count(array_filter($users, static fn($row) => ($row['role'] ?? '') === 'siswa'));
$data_user_error = !$result;

$e = static function ($value): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string) $value, ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
};

$role_labels = [
    'admin' => 'Administrator',
    'guru' => 'Guru',
    'siswa' => 'Siswa',
];
$role_pills = [
    'admin' => 'is-red',
    'guru' => 'is-blue',
    'siswa' => 'is-green',
];
$role_icons = [
    'admin' => 'bi-shield-lock-fill',
    'guru' => 'bi-person-workspace',
    'siswa' => 'bi-mortarboard-fill',
];

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/views/data_user_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

</file>

<file path="admin/data_mapel.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';
check_role(['admin']);

$e = static function ($value): string {
    return htmlspecialchars((string) ($value ?? ''), ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
};

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['aksi'])) {
    $aksi = (string) $_POST['aksi'];

    if ($aksi === 'tambah' || $aksi === 'edit') {
        $id_mapel = (int) ($_POST['id_mapel'] ?? 0);
        $kode = trim((string) ($_POST['kode_mapel'] ?? ''));
        $nama = trim((string) ($_POST['nama_mapel'] ?? ''));
        $guru_id = (int) ($_POST['guru_id'] ?? 0);

        if ($kode === '' || $nama === '' || $guru_id <= 0 || ($aksi === 'edit' && $id_mapel <= 0)) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Lengkapi kode, nama mata pelajaran, dan guru pengampu dengan benar.');
        } else {
            $stmt_guru = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'SELECT id FROM guru WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1');
            $guru_valid = false;
            if ($stmt_guru) {
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt_guru, 'i', $guru_id);
                mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt_guru);
                $guru_result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt_guru);
                $guru_valid = $guru_result && mysqli_num_rows($guru_result) === 1;
                mysqli_stmt_close($stmt_guru);
            }

            if (!$guru_valid) {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Guru pengampu yang dipilih tidak ditemukan.');
            } else {
                $stmt_cek = mysqli_prepare(
                    $conn,
                    $aksi === 'edit'
                        ? 'SELECT id FROM mapel WHERE kode_mapel = ? AND id <> ? LIMIT 1'
                        : 'SELECT id FROM mapel WHERE kode_mapel = ? LIMIT 1'
                );
                $kode_dipakai = false;
                $cek_kode_gagal = !$stmt_cek;

                if ($stmt_cek) {
                    if ($aksi === 'edit') {
                        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt_cek, 'si', $kode, $id_mapel);
                    } else {
                        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt_cek, 's', $kode);
                    }
                    if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt_cek)) {
                        $kode_result = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt_cek);
                        $kode_dipakai = $kode_result && mysqli_num_rows($kode_result) > 0;
                    } else {
                        $cek_kode_gagal = true;
                    }
                    mysqli_stmt_close($stmt_cek);
                }

                if ($cek_kode_gagal) {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Pemeriksaan kode mata pelajaran gagal. Silakan coba kembali.');
                } elseif ($kode_dipakai) {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Kode mata pelajaran sudah digunakan. Gunakan kode yang berbeda.');
                } elseif ($aksi === 'tambah') {
                    $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'INSERT INTO mapel (kode_mapel, nama_mapel, guru_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)');
                    if ($stmt) {
                        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'ssi', $kode, $nama, $guru_id);
                        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
                            set_flash_message('success', 'Mata pelajaran berhasil ditambahkan!');
                        } else {
                            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menambahkan mata pelajaran. Periksa kembali data yang dimasukkan.');
                        }
                        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
                    } else {
                        set_flash_message('danger', 'Data mata pelajaran tidak dapat disimpan saat ini.');
                    }
                } else {
                    $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'UPDATE mapel SET kode_mapel = ?, nama_mapel = ?, guru_id = ? WHERE id = ?');
                    if ($stmt) {
                        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'ssii', $kode, $nama, $guru_id, $id_mapel);
                        if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt)) {
                            set_flash_message('success', 'Mata pelajaran berhasil diperbarui!');
                        } else {
                            set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal memperbarui mata pelajaran.');
                        }
                        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
                    } else {
                        set_flash_message('danger', 'Perubahan mata pelajaran tidak dapat diproses saat ini.');
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    } elseif ($aksi === 'hapus') {
        $id_mapel = (int) ($_POST['id_mapel'] ?? 0);
        if ($id_mapel <= 0) {
            set_flash_message('danger', 'Mata pelajaran yang dipilih tidak valid.');
        } else {
            $stmt = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'DELETE FROM mapel WHERE id = ?');
            if ($stmt) {
                mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt, 'i', $id_mapel);
                if (mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt) && mysqli_stmt_affected_rows($stmt) > 0) {
                    set_flash_message('success', 'Mata pelajaran berhasil dihapus!');
                } else {
                    set_flash_message('danger', 'Gagal menghapus mata pelajaran. Pastikan mata pelajaran tidak sedang digunakan pada penugasan atau nilai rapor.');
                }
                mysqli_stmt_close($stmt);
            } else {
                set_flash_message('danger', 'Permintaan penghapusan mata pelajaran tidak dapat diproses.');
            }
        }
    } else {
        set_flash_message('danger', 'Aksi mata pelajaran tidak dikenali.');
    }

    header('Location: data_mapel.php');
    exit();
}

// Ambil guru untuk pilihan formulir.
$guru_list = [];
$guru_result = mysqli_query($conn, 'SELECT id, nama FROM guru ORDER BY nama ASC');
if ($guru_result) {
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($guru_result)) {
        $guru_list[] = $row;
    }
}

// Jumlah peserta didik dihitung dari relasi mengajar pada setiap mata pelajaran.
$query = "
    SELECT
        m.id,
        m.kode_mapel,
        m.nama_mapel,
        m.guru_id,
        g.nama AS nama_guru,
        COUNT(DISTINCT mg.siswa_id) AS total_siswa
    FROM mapel m
    LEFT JOIN guru g ON m.guru_id = g.id
    LEFT JOIN mengajar mg ON mg.mapel_id = m.id
    GROUP BY m.id, m.kode_mapel, m.nama_mapel, m.guru_id, g.nama
    ORDER BY m.kode_mapel ASC, m.nama_mapel ASC
";
$result_mapel = mysqli_query($conn, $query);
$mapel_list = [];
if ($result_mapel) {
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result_mapel)) {
        $mapel_list[] = $row;
    }
}

$total_mapel = count($mapel_list);
$guru_terlibat = [];
$total_penugasan = 0;
foreach ($mapel_list as $row) {
    if (!empty($row['guru_id'])) {
        $guru_terlibat[(int) $row['guru_id']] = true;
    }
    $total_penugasan += (int) $row['total_siswa'];
}
$jumlah_guru_pengampu = count($guru_terlibat);
$query_error = !$guru_result || !$result_mapel;

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/views/data_mapel_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

</file>

<file path="admin/riwayat.php">
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/koneksi_database.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_fungsi.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/helper_cek_login.php';

check_role(['admin']);

$escape = static function ($value) {
    return htmlspecialchars((string) ($value ?? ''), ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8');
};

// Halaman ini mengikuti sumber data yang tersedia pada proyek: akun terbaru
// dari tabel users, diurutkan berdasarkan ID. Ini bukan audit log aktivitas.
$summary_query = mysqli_query($conn, "
    SELECT
        COUNT(*) AS total,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN role = 'admin' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS total_admin,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN role = 'guru' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS total_guru,
        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN role = 'siswa' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS total_siswa
    FROM users
");

$summary = $summary_query ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($summary_query) : null;

$query_riwayat = mysqli_query(
    $conn,
    'SELECT id, username, role FROM users ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50'
);

$riwayat = [];
if ($query_riwayat) {
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($query_riwayat)) {
        $riwayat[] = $row;
    }
}

$query_error = !$summary_query || !$query_riwayat;
$total_ditampilkan = count($riwayat);

$role_config = [
    'admin' => [
        'label' => 'Administrator',
        'class' => 'is-admin',
        'icon'  => 'bi-shield-lock-fill',
    ],
    'guru' => [
        'label' => 'Guru',
        'class' => 'is-guru',
        'icon'  => 'bi-person-workspace',
    ],
    'siswa' => [
        'label' => 'Siswa',
        'class' => 'is-siswa',
        'icon'  => 'bi-mortarboard-fill',
    ],
];

require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_header.php';
require __DIR__ . '/views/riwayat_view.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../includes/template_footer.php';

<file path="includes/components/navigation/role_links.php">
<?php
/**
 * Konfigurasi tautan navigasi berdasarkan role.
 * Data ini dipakai oleh sidebar dan topbar agar rute tidak ditulis dua kali.
 */
$role_navigation_items = [];
$topbar_navigation_items = [];

if ($current_role === 'siswa') {
    $role_navigation_items = [
        [
            'href'  => BASE_URL . 'siswa/dashboard_siswa.php',
            'page'  => 'dashboard_siswa.php',
            'title' => 'Beranda',
            'label' => 'Beranda',
            'icon'  => 'bi-house-door',
        ],
        [
            'href'  => BASE_URL . 'siswa/lihat_nilai_rapor.php',
            'page'  => 'lihat_nilai_rapor.php',
            'title' => 'Lihat Nilai Rapor',
            'label' => 'Lihat Nilai Rapor',
            'icon'  => 'bi-file-earmark-text',
        ],
    ];
    $topbar_navigation_items = $role_navigation_items;
} elseif ($current_role === 'guru') {
    $role_navigation_items = [
        [
            'href'  => BASE_URL . 'guru/dashboard_guru.php',
            'page'  => 'dashboard_guru.php',
            'title' => 'Beranda',
            'label' => 'Beranda',
            'icon'  => 'bi-house-door',
        ],
        [
            'href'  => BASE_URL . 'guru/data_nilai_siswa.php',
            'page'  => 'data_nilai_siswa.php',
            'title' => 'Kelola Nilai Siswa',
            'label' => 'Kelola Nilai Siswa',
            'icon'  => 'bi-pencil-square',
        ],
    ];
    $topbar_navigation_items = $role_navigation_items;
} elseif ($current_role === 'admin') {
    // Topbar admin hanya menampilkan tautan Beranda, sesuai perilaku sebelumnya.
    $topbar_navigation_items = [
        [
            'href'  => BASE_URL . 'admin/dashboard_admin.php',
            'page'  => 'dashboard_admin.php',
            'title' => 'Beranda',
            'label' => 'Beranda',
            'icon'  => 'bi-house-door',
        ],
    ];
}

$role_label_map = [
    'admin' => 'Administrator',
    'guru'  => 'Guru',
    'siswa' => 'Siswa',
];
$current_role_label = $role_label_map[$current_role] ?? 'Tamu';

</file>

<file path="includes/template_header.php">
<?php
// Pastikan session sudah aktif.
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

$current_role     = $_SESSION['role'] ?? 'guest';
$current_username = $_SESSION['username'] ?? 'Pengguna';
$current_page     = basename($_SERVER['PHP_SELF'] ?? '');

$is_dashboard_page = in_array($current_page, [
    'dashboard_admin.php',
    'dashboard_guru.php',
    'dashboard_siswa.php',
], true);
// Dashboard menggunakan staggered entrance setiap kali halaman beranda dibuka.
// Flag lama dari proses login tetap dikonsumsi agar tidak tertinggal di session.
$animate_dashboard_entry = $is_dashboard_page;
if ($is_dashboard_page && isset($_SESSION['sipn_dashboard_entry'])) {
    unset($_SESSION['sipn_dashboard_entry']);
}

// Ambil foto profil pengguna yang sedang login.
$current_foto = null;
if (isset($_SESSION['user_id'], $conn)) {
    $user_id_header = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
    $stmt_foto = mysqli_prepare($conn, 'SELECT foto FROM users WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1');

    if ($stmt_foto) {
        mysqli_stmt_bind_param($stmt_foto, 'i', $user_id_header);
        mysqli_stmt_execute($stmt_foto);
        $result_foto = mysqli_stmt_get_result($stmt_foto);

        if ($result_foto && ($data_foto = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result_foto))) {
            $current_foto = $data_foto['foto'] ?? null;
        }

        mysqli_stmt_close($stmt_foto);
    }
}

$profile_image_path = __DIR__ . '/../assets/img/profil/' . basename((string) $current_foto);
$has_profile_image = !empty($current_foto) && is_file($profile_image_path);

// Satu sumber data navigasi untuk sidebar dan topbar.
require_once __DIR__ . '/components/navigation/role_links.php';

// Kerangka halaman dipisahkan ke partial agar mudah dirawat.
require_once __DIR__ . '/layouts/document_head.php';
?>
<div class="app-layout-wrapper">
    <?php require __DIR__ . '/components/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="app-main-content" id="appMainContent">
        <?php require __DIR__ . '/components/topbar.php'; ?>

        <main class="page-container" id="mainContent">
            <?php display_flash_message(); ?>

</file>

<file path="includes/layouts/document_head.php">

<html lang="id">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <meta name="theme-color" content="#1e3a8a">
    <title>SIPN - Sistem Informasi Pengelolaan Nilai | SMK Sangkuriang 1 Cimahi</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap-icons@1.11.1/font/bootstrap-icons.css">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="<?= BASE_URL; ?>assets/css/style.css?v=sipn-modular-css-20261114">
    <!-- Satu entry point JavaScript; fitur dipisah ke modules/. -->
    <script type="module" src="<?= BASE_URL; ?>assets/js/sipn-modules.js?v=sipn-js-modular-20261115"></script>
</head>
<body data-theme="light" class="<?= $animate_dashboard_entry ? 'sipn-dashboard-stagger' : ''; ?>">

</file>
